<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * with a value axis, gridlines, a hover crosshair + tooltip and a legend that</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *   &#x3C;TrendChart</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *     series={[{ id: "pro", label: "Pro" }, { id: "team", label: "Team" }]}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Colours default to the chart palette (chart-1…); pass `color` per series to</span></span>
